boolean-demo #sleela 2.5 (lib/boolean/boolean-demo.sleela)

BooleanDemo class

Runnable demonstrator for the boolean package — a Return Value may plainly imply TRUE/FALSE, or it may imply a further relation. This program reconstructs, inline, the HEAD (result) + CARRY (caveat) reading of Boolean/Binary syllable words at depth 8, 16, and 32.

Carries the standard Metadata Class record — eleven catalogued provenance attributes (version, id, author, date, copyright, trademark, corporation, origin, birth date, science rating, national schedule) with matching get/set accessors. These are omitted from the tables below for brevity.

BooleanWord16 #sleela 2.5 (lib/boolean/16/BooleanWord16.sleela)

BooleanWord16 class

Defines BooleanWord16, the generative factory for all 65536 depth-16 Boolean/Binary words. Full materialization of every combination as a file is impractical at this depth; this factory models any such word by its HEAD (result) and CARRY (caveat) composition, which is validated and read on demand.

Carries the standard Metadata Class record — eleven catalogued provenance attributes (version, id, author, date, copyright, trademark, corporation, origin, birth date, science rating, national schedule) with matching get/set accessors. These are omitted from the tables below for brevity.

FieldDeclarationWhat it holds
headBitint headBit1 = TRUE result, 0 = FALSE result
carryTrueint carryTruenumber of carried TRUE syllables
carryFalseint carryFalsenumber of carried FALSE syllables

Methods — the actions this unit can perform.

MethodSignatureWhat it does

depthint depth()No description in source.
carryDepthint carryDepth()No description in source.
validboolean valid()Valid when the head bit is 0/1 and the carry syllables fill the carry slots.

headSyllableString headSyllable()No description in source.
caveatboolean caveat()A caveat exists when any carried syllable is FALSE.

falseCountint falseCount()No description in source.
